- **fable-replace**: Verified, transactional find/replace for code and docs. Use INSTEAD of sed, perl, python s.replace() or repeated Edit calls whenever a change spans more than one file or more than one spot: a rename across 50 files, a comment sweep, several dependent edits in one file that must all land or none, an insert under a line, a new file.
